## `AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Gas-Spring-Safety Tester
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Furniture Surface Coating Hardness & Cabinet Static Load Stability
- **Intro:** Gas-Spring-Safety Tester — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Furniture Surface Coating Hardness & Cabinet Static Load Stability — Pre-Operational Verification and Parameter Baseline: Ambient temperature: 23±2°C; Relative humidity: 50±5% RH; Gas spring preload: 120±2 bar; Pressure gauge accuracy: ±0.5% FS.
- **Key tolerance:** Ambient temperature: 23±2°C; Relative humidity: 50±5% RH; Gas spring preload: 120±2 bar; Pressure gauge accuracy: ±0.5% FS.
- **Standards:** `ISO 2409:2020`, `ISO 4211-2:2017`, `ANSI/BIFMA X5.1-2017 General-Purpose Office Seating - Static Loa`, `ASTM D4366-16 Standard Test Methods for Hardness of Organic Coat`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-3100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
